# Stock, ETF and index logos

One plain mark per non-crypto row of the instrument table (`SYMBOLS` in
`src/pages/strategies/strategy.ts`), drawn by `AssetIcon` on a white disc with 14%
padding (`object-contain`) and the shared rim, so every mark reads on the light and the
dark theme. They ship with the app as hashed files; nothing is loaded from a third-party
host. `AssetIcon.test.tsx` fails if a stock row has no file here, or a file here is not
listed below.

The listed Asian stocks (the API's listed instruments, by Yahoo ticker) are not `SYMBOLS`
rows: a listed symbol may have a file here, recorded in `listed-logos.json`, and every
listed symbol without one is recorded there as a monogram with its reason (section
"Listed Asian stocks" below).

**Company logos are trademarks of their owners.** They are used only to identify the
listed security, and imply no endorsement, affiliation or index-provider licence. The
licences below cover the drawings, not the marks.

## Convention (for every wave that adds a stock)

- **File:** `{ID}.svg` (preferred) or `{ID}.png`; `ID` is the universe symbol,
  upper-case. A PNG preserves its source proportions, with a longest side of at least
  80 native px and a shortest side of at least 16 px. Prefer about 40 KB or less;
  verified official files may be up to 128 KB. The component contains wordmarks in
  its square disc just as it does non-square SVGs; never stretch a wordmark square.
- **Content:** the plain mark, not pre-rounded and without its own disc or tile;
  `AssetIcon` adds the disc. The viewBox is tight to the mark.
- **Mark:** prefer a clear emblem or symbol. An authentic wordmark is also used when
  no verified standalone emblem is available; preserve its proportions and colors.
- **Clean SVG:** no scripts, event handlers, external references, styles, titles,
  comments or editor metadata (checked by the unit test).
- **Source priority:** (1) openly licensed icon sets (Simple Icons CC0, or a ticker-logo
  collection with a verified MIT or CC licence file); (2) Wikimedia Commons, with the
  file page and licence; (3) the company's official site or press kit; (4) a public
  keyless logo CDN, recorded as the source.
- **Index or ETF rows** with no single company use the flag of the exposure country
  (flag-icons, MIT).
- **Identity:** check the company, e.g. Hyundai Motor (005380), not Hyundai Heavy; SK
  hynix, not SK Telecom.

## Files

Fetched 2026-10-06. Simple Icons is the npm tarball
[`simple-icons-16.34.0.tgz`](https://registry.npmjs.org/simple-icons/-/simple-icons-16.34.0.tgz)
(SHA256 `25887ddf96a084a82f9181dc3a7e99750d6b5ac4339fe9321d0e2bf14669dc65`), path
`package/icons/{slug}.svg`, fill from the brand hex in its `data/simple-icons.json`.
flag-icons is [`flag-icons-7.5.0.tgz`](https://registry.npmjs.org/flag-icons/-/flag-icons-7.5.0.tgz)
(SHA256 `c0b80bf0e08006a60f56621d6bc49f8c7131f4d1fef6737a165a673431f4b518`), path
`package/flags/4x3/{cc}.svg`. Commons files matched the SHA1 that Commons publishes for them.

Every SVG keeps the source's path geometry; role, title, comments and editor metadata
are removed and the viewBox is set tight to the mark. The CC0 text is in
[notices/simple-icons-CC0-1.0.md](notices/simple-icons-CC0-1.0.md).

## Listed Asian stocks (files named by Yahoo ticker)

The current API catalogue contains 604 listed Asian stocks, named by their upper-case Yahoo ticker.
All 604 now have a bundled company logo. The first 2026-10-13 pass supplied 199; the
2026-10-20 completion pass added 405. The original
source records below are retained, followed by the completion pass and its full provenance ledger.

Marks identify the issuer, checked against current official artwork or corroborating sources.
Photographs, unrelated companies, obsolete brand identities and stretched copies are rejected.
A shared group emblem is used only when the issuer uses it in its own corporate identity.
Authentic wordmarks keep their original proportions; source-native PNGs are not upscaled.

The manifest `listed-logos.json` records each bundled file. Initials remain a fallback for unknown
instruments and failed image loads, rather than an intentionally omitted catalogue logo.

## Earlier waves (superseded)

Round 7 bundled NVDA, TSLA, AAPL, GOOGL and META as Simple Icons marks and MSFT and AMZN
as monochrome Font Awesome (CC BY 4.0) drawings, each pre-composed on its own white
disc, and SP500 and XYZ100 as original glyphs. The Asia wave added BABA,
HYUNDAI and SMSN the same way (SMSN on a blue disc) under `src/assets/coins`. Its note
that Simple Icons has no Zhipu icon was wrong (`zdotai` is Z.ai, Zhipu's international
brand), and it left TSM, SKHX, SOFTBANK, MINIMAX, ZHIPU, EWJ and EWY on a monogram. All
of these were replaced by the files above; the Font Awesome drawings are no longer
bundled.
